ki shah-e-husn par zill-e-huma hai
tirgi TuuT paDi hai mujh par
sulh men chheD hai laDai ki
ye kah gae hain musafir luTe gharon vaale
na sahi jaur magar jaur ka iqrar to hai
kabhi shakh o sabza o barg par kabhi ghuncha o gul o khaar par
jo tu dariya-e-mai hai to main khamyaza huun sahil ka
kis ka sahra kaisa gulshan
jis rizq se aati ho parvaz men kotahi
vafa kar ke bhi ham to sharma rahe hain
ai tira ghamza yak-qalam-angez
baat sunna na kisi chahne vaale ki kabhi
shaam paDte hi kisi shakhs ki yaad

aap be-bahra hai jo mo'taqid-e-'mir' nahin
ab to is raah se vo shakhs guzarta bhi nahin
jis hasin se hui ulfat vahi maashuq apna
ek siita thi jo satai gai

ik umanDti hui ghaTa tha firaq

rahravo ba-khabar raho ki guman
koi apni hi nazar se to hamen dekhega

ab itni sadgi laaen kahan se

sadgi o purkari be-khudi o hushyari

aisa miTha tira andaz tha farmane ka

ai sanam tujh jabin upar ye khaal

aaj sarsabz koh o sahra hai

phir un se na milne ki qasam kha to gae ham

meri tasvir bhi girti to chhanaka hota

bas-ki roka main ne aur siine men ubhrin pai-ba-pai
ab to ham baat bhi karte nahin gham-khvar ke saath
uTha ke hathon se tum ne chhoDa chalo na danista tum ne toDa

vo qatl bhi karte hain to charcha nahin hota
kabhi 'faraz' nae mausamon men ro dena

jo sukut-e-raz rangin dastan banta gaya
yaqin hai ki ho jaae akhir ko sachchi

socha hai khamoshi se har ik zahr ko pi jaaen
kal ka har vaqia tumhara tha

tu baDa tha tujhe baDa maana

tumhin munsifi se kah do tumhen e'tibar hota
har vaqt ik khumar tha har dam surur tha

khud chali aai miri hausla-afzai ko
ziyarat-gah-e-ahl-e-azm-o-himmat hai lahad meri
'ghalib' ye khauf hai ki kahan se ada karun

honTon ko vo karte nahin tar ab-e-baqa se

apne haq men dua kare koi

lutf gaya ehsan gaya inaam gaya ikram gaya
panja-e-ishq ne betab kiya jab suun mujhe
ham ne maana ki mila mulk-e-jahan
sikha deti hain chalna Thokaren bhi rahgiron ko
the bure mughbachon ke tevar lek
mere pate se khalq ko kyuun tera ghar mile
isi kamre men chhup gaya hai kahin
suna hai chashm-e-tasavvur se dasht-e-imkan men
tumhara tir-e-hasrat ban gaya dil men nihan ho kar
vaqt ke tiir to siine pe sambhale ham ne
vo khat ke purze uDa raha tha
dekhna lala-e-khud-rav ka lahakna saaqi
hava ki chaap se shaklen ubharna hongi
ye jaan to aani jaani hai is jaan ki to koi baat nahin
use kashti nahin milti ise sahil nahin milta
vaqt paDne pe hathon se jaate rahe
bahar kaun si saughat le ke aai hai
nashsha men gum-karda-rah aaya vo mast-e-fitna-khu
phir sab se ye kah ki main khuda huun
phir aa kar tumhare qadam dekhte hain
sarab huun main tiri pyaas kya bujhaungi
kahan gae vo sakhi sabz chadaron vaale
main khud ye chahta huun ki halat huun kharab
junun kar-farma hua chahta hai
kal jo tha aaj vo mizaj nahin
lutf-e-soz-o-gudaz kya janen
khud bhi aata hai apne dhyan men kya
himmat-e-qatil baDhana chahiye
jaise ho phir bhi achchhe ho
mujh ko bhi shauq tha nae chehron ki diid ka
lage hai kanTa nazar men sona kaTengi kaise ye kaali ratiyan
ham dekhte hain tum men khuda jaane buto kya
main jurm ka e'tiraf kar ke
aadhi soi aadhi jaagi thaki do-pahri jaisi maan
varna uDa ke paan nasha-e-banj le chala
is zamin o asman ko be-karan samjha tha main
magar rind us ko vali jante hain
garche dast-o-pa ko aksar be-khata paate hain ham
thi fughan vo bhi jise zabt-e-fughan samjha tha main
hasrat shabab ki hai ayyam-e-sheb men bhi
jahan-e-raz hui ja rahi hai aankh tiri
nigah-e-yar ko ab kyuun hai taghaful ai dil
saal bhar ke baad aai ai saba achchhi tarah
use pata hi nahin hai ki pyaar ki baazi
hasad saza-e-kamal-e-sukhan hai kya kijiye
allah teri shaan ke qurban jaiye
batao us mizha ko dekh kar ki mujh ko qarar
aaj soe hain tah-e-khak na jaane yahan kitne
vaaiz ik aib se tu paak hai ya zat-e-khuda
zakhm ankhon ke bhi sahte the kabhi dil vaale
taajjub aata hai tifl-e-dil par ki ho gaya mast-e-nazm-e-'akbar'
kis bahane se koi shahr jalaya jaae
nasir-e-daulat-o-din aur muin-e-millat-o-mulk
jise zid ho husn ke zikr se jise chiDh ho ishq ke naam se
hazaron men ye ek dam dekhte hain
tavaf-e-manzil-e-janan hamen bhi karna hai
ghanim sab ka yunhi bechta rahega lahu
hai ye takiya tiri ataon par
majburi o dava-e-giraftari-e-ulfat
hosh aarif ki hai yahi pahchan
ki saaya phailta jaata hai juun juun dhuup Dhalti hai
aaj pardes men ghar yaad aaya
'vasim' ham bhi bikharne ka hausla karte
jism ki har baat hai avargi ye mat kaho
kab kisi dar ki jabha-sai ki
hazaron saal biite hain hazaron saal bitenge
zamin ne khuun ugla asman ne aag barsai
abhi kuchh log baaqi hain jahan men
vaaiz ki baat rah gai saaqi ki chal gai
yaani mere baad bhi yaani saans liye jaate honge
dil-e-shaida ne paaya ishq men mearaj ka rutba
ne bhagne ki guun na iqamat ki taab hai
tu ne gar ai ashiq-e-dil-gir phir khinchi to kya
ru-ba-ru gar hoga yusuf aur tu aa jaega
nuqsan nahin junun se jo sauda kare koi
aur is sahra men tera duur tak saaya nahin
akhir apna kaha kiya tu ne
jis ke hote hue hote the zamane mere
shahr se bahar ki virani basana thi mujhe
dil le ke kahte hain tiri khatir se le liya
dekh kar joban tira kis kis ko hairani hui
din ka kya zikr tira-bakhton men
haae kya ho gaya tabiat ko
bacha liya harzagi se us ne lihaz-e-namus-o-nang ho kar
chuum kar phuul ko ahista se
is ko kahte hain alam-arai
bahar-e-mai-kashi aai chaman ki rut badalti hai
hangame jaag uThte hain aksar ghuTan ke baad
yak-bakht auj nazr-e-subuk-bari-e-'asad'
tu ne Dhala hai aur Dhale hain ham
gareban men ulajhne se tabiat to bahalti hai
ujala ho to ham ankhen jhapakte rahte hain
samaaton men ghane jangalon ki sansen hain
us maqam-e-qarb tak ab ishq pahuncha hi jahan
hai nama-bar ko us se dava-e-ham-kalami
ki ek khema-e-jan tha so be-tanab hua
jab chale aae chaman-zar se ham
be-navai tar sada-e-naghma-e-shohrat 'asad'
ashiq ke marne ka andaz hai sarapa
shama ki raushni zabani hai
shamil kisi ka khun-e-tamanna zarur tha
hasil na kiije dahr se ibrat hi kyuun na ho
ishq ko hausla hai shart arna
fana isi rang par hai qaaim falak vahi chaal chal raha hai
jaan de de chal ke dar par yaar ke
jo baDh jaate hain had se vo hi ghaT kar kam bhi hote hain
na khaDe ho jiye khuban-e-dil-azar ke paas
khatm kiya saba ne raqs gul pe nisar ho chuki
apni ghairat bech Dalen apna maslak chhoD den
main chup karata huun har shab umaDti barish ko
kahte hain jab rahi na mujhe taqat-e-sukhan
jab naam tira lijiye tab chashm bhar aave
itne khaif kyuun rahte ho
vo sasural se aai hai maike
aao mil jaao muskura ke gale
'zafar' logon ke dikhlane ko yaksui se kya hasil
be-takalluf aae tum khole hue band-e-qaba
ret hi ret hai is dil men musafir mere
kiije kis munh se in khataon par
jo dil men hai use ankhon se kahlana zaruri hai
nahin beganagi achchhi rafiq-e-rah-e-manzil se
ye nahin daryaft karte kis ne is ki jaan li
vo jab bhi karte hain is nutq o lab ki bakhiya-gari
kahan pe laai hai tum ko ye zindagi dekho
phir kho na jaaen ham kahin duniya ki bhiiD men
apni girah se kuchh na mujhe aap dijiye
mere chehre pe tira naam na paDh le koi
aap jaana udhar aur aap hi hairan hona
jis dil pe naaz tha mujhe vo dil nahin raha
na sile ki na sataish ki tamanna ham ko
ham-safar chahiye hujum nahin
vo apni khu na chhoDenge ham apni vaza kyuun chhoDen
mujhe maalum hai jo tu ne mere haq men socha hai
aag se paani men bujhte vaqt uThti hai sada
kabhi yak-ba-yak jo chhalak gai kisi rind-e-mast ke jaam se
sahiban-e-kitab hain ham log
girah men le ke gareban ka taar taar chale
tu dua de mire afsana-e-rusvai ko
jo ghar men puchhe koi khauf kya hai kah dena
magar tumhare taghaful ne hoshyar kiya
yaad ham koi dam nahin karte
dil idhar hota hai zalim na udhar hota hai
tu husn haur ada men ejaaz hai sarapa
jazba-e-dil ne mire tasir dikhlai to hai
paani ke bulbule ki tarah se miTa diya
jaur ta hadd-e-naz ho jaae
ishq minnat-e-kash-e-qarar nahin
aghaz hi likha gaya anjam rah gaya
vahshat-e-shor-e-tamasha hai ki juun nikhat-e-gul
hamesha tishna-e-ab-e-baqa huun
hava-e-subh yak-alam gareban chaki-e-gul hai
pa-e-afgar pe jab se tujhe rahm aaya hai
kiya jiyun lafz men maani sirijan
aage aage dekhiye hota hai kya
huun charaghan-e-havas juun kaghaz-e-atish-zada
ai tira zulm sar-ba-sar andaz
kya ham us bad-guman se baat karen
dost ko yuun bacha diya tu ne
ye ishq nahin asan itna hi samajh liije
jab aankh khul gai na ziyan tha na suud tha
niind men Duubi hui chandr-kiran kya kahna
parvanon ne jalna chhoD diya shamon ne pighalna chhoD diya
tujh nain ka khayal mujhe jam-e-jam hua
mauj-e-sad-iztirab hona tha
taghaful-ha-e-saqi ka gila kya
tiri gali men bahae phire hai sail-e-sarishk
dil se le arsh tak bhara hai ishq
ang ang apna isi rut men mahakta dekhun
tabiat apni ghabrati hai jab sunsan raton men
hamesha fitne hi barpa kiye mire sar par
hai zulekha khush ki mahv-e-mah-e-kanan ho gaiin
sar-basta har ik khar-e-bayaban hai maujud
ishq hai ishq karne valon ko
tum se mil kar bahut khushi ho kya
silsila fitna-e-qayamat ka
tab chak-e-gareban ka maza hai dil-e-nalan
ye shamat aai ki us ki gali men dil ne kaha
vaah kya munh se phuul jhaDte hain
mere hathon men mira ghar to hai darvaza nahin
miri qimat miri guftar se puchh
nazar aati nahin ab dil men tamanna koi
sharab-e-shauq sen sarshar hain ham
faqat ye baat ki pir-e-mughan hai mard-e-khaliq
goya ki do aalam se 'zafar' be-khabari di
na aenge vo raah par dekh lena
jis men firdaus ke nazare hain
ajza-e-nala dil men mire rizq-e-ham hue
mil jaae najat ishq ko is dar-ba-dari se
har shab piya hi karte hain mai jis qadar mile
phir hua vaqt ki ho bal-kusha mauj-e-sharab
taqaza-e-digar hai aur main huun
din nahin badlega tarikh badal jaegi
khirman jale agar na malakh khaae kisht ko
ya mere zakhm-e-rashk ko rusva na kijiye
tu dil-e-be-sabr ko taskin zara si de gaya
is bhare shahr men raegan ek main
kabhi dil ko sahara de gaya hai
us pe takrar bhi karte ho kharidar ke saath
har gosha-e-bisat hai sar shisha-baz ka
ai khuda dard-e-dil hai bakhshish-e-dost
tu nahin jab to vahan kya hoga
lekin jab ham mil jaate hain kho jaata hai jaane kaun
aa ki tujh bin bahut dinon se ye dil
dam-e-taqrir khil uThte hain gulistan kya kya
rahmat hogi talib-e-isyan
par tumhen sharmsar kaun kare
puchhta hai haath men mere bata kya chiiz hai
ki sab be-ghar hon aur mera ho ghar achchha nahin lagta
ruuh ko bhi maza mohabbat ka
mamura-e-ehsas men hai hashr sa barpa
kise nahin hai tamanna-e-sarvari lekin
bichhaDne vaale men sab kuchh thaa bevafai na thi
mauj-e-sharab yak-mizha-e-khvab-nak hai
ai safar itna raegan to na ja
mat mil ahl-e-duval ke laDkon se
sar se guzre pe bhi hai bal-e-huma mauj-e-sharab
kyuun miri gham-khvargi ka tujh ko aaya tha khayal
jazbat bhi hindu hote hain chahat bhi musalman hoti hai
'asad' hai nazaa men chal bevafa bara-e-khuda
do din ki zindagi men hazaron baras jiye
apne men gar shamma-e-mehr-o-vafa paate hain ham
dil bhi maana nahin ki tujh se kahen
iman ki to ye hai ki iman to gaya
qadam rakkha ki manzil rasta thi
ye barhaman ka karam vo ata-e-shaikh-e-haram
dekhen dil ka shikar kaun kare
is rang se uThai kal us ne 'asad' ki naash
kaash ifsha-e-raz ho jaae
hazar aina dil bandhe hai bal-e-yak-tapidan par
kisi ko apna kahun koi mujh ko apna le
ye sard rut ye sard hava us gali men chal
ajab kya gar mah o parvin mire nakhchir ho jaaen
mai vo kyuun bahut piite bazm-e-ghair men ya rab
ashiq ke huquq aa kar nahaq bhi miTa jaana
kaise buland-o-bala shajar khaak ho gae
jab hota hai koi hamdam hota hai
ki ye duniya hui purani bhi
apne insaf ki zanjir na dekho ki yahan
meri aahen bakhiya-e-chak-e-gareban ho gaiin
ab-o-dana nahin ki tujh se kahen
bahut bad-hal hain basti tire log
faqat ik mera naam tha mera
yaad aate hain moajize apne
ek ka naam navazinda tha aur ik ka bazinda tha
peD ke kaTne valon ko ye malum to tha
daur-e-tarab guzar gaya amad-e-yar ho chuki
diya apni khudi ko jo ham ne uTha vo jo parda sa biich men tha na raha
ye uThna baiThna mahfil men un ka rang laega
kahan pahunch gai duniya use pata hi nahin
vajd men laate hain mujh ko bulbulon ke zamzame
zikr hai kuchh gila nahin baat hai neshtar nahin
chalo vo kufr ke ghar se salamat aa gae lekin
saari baton ki safai di hai
un ka jo farz hai vo ahl-e-siyasat janen
mere huzur shama ne girya jo sar kiya
maulvi sahib na chhoDenge khuda go bakhsh de
khvabida ba-hairat-kada-e-dagh hain aahen
ilaj-e-zof-e-yaqin in se ho nahin sakta
aina aah mere muqabil nahin raha
dard-o-gham-e-firaq ke ye sakht marhale
vo zard patte jo peD se TuuT kar gire the
shaam hoti hai to vahshat nahin dekhi jaati
baiTha likkhun kaghaz par
us mauj-khez dahr men ham ko qaza ne aah
khat paDhte hue aTak raha hai
hai vo bechargi ka haal ki ham
parde parde men mohabbat dushman-e-jani hui
ab ke bahar ne bhi kiin aisi shararten ki bas
par nahin zahid koi TaTTi ki aaD
janab-e-shaikh apna rasta len
jo imtihan-e-taba kare apna ai 'zafar'
hamin ko nazm-e-gulistan pe ikhtiyar nahin
yad-gar-e-nala ik divan-e-be-shiraza tha
hamen bhi atish-e-ulfat jala chuki 'akbar'
us ne jo aag laga di vo bujhai na gai
meri nazren bujh gaiin ya teri raanai gai
muntazir kis ka huun TuuTi hui dahliz pe main
vo mile to ye puchhna hai mujhe
gar vo sada samai hai chang o rabab men
khizr phir to chashma-e-ab-e-baqa kya chiiz hai
rakhta ho misl-e-saya-e-gul sar-ba-pa-e-gul
sitam itna to na kiije ki uTha bhi na sakun
sharbat hi batate hain sam kah nahin sakte
tarik raston men kahin kho na jaaen ham
jab bhi gae azab-e-dar-o-bam tha vahi
mera na ek haath na us ke hazar haath
ye dosti ye marasim ye chahaten ye khulus
phuvaren si naghmon ki paDti hon jaise
ki jo khaliq ki taraf dil ko jhuka hi na sake
main jo tha us gali ka mast-e-khiram
ta ham ko shikayat ki bhi baaqi na rahe ja
ki bar lavega matlab kuun khuda ahista ahista
soz-e-raqabat paida kar ke us ki niind uDaai hai
jab ki vaan umr bhar gaya huun main
aaya na raas nala-e-dil ka asar mujhe
dekhi vafa-e-fursat-e-ranj-o-nashat-e-dahr
kitne nadan hain tire bhulne vaale ki tujhe
faqih-e-shahr qarun hai lughat-ha-e-hijazi ka
mujh ko to gham ne fursat-e-gham bhi na di 'firaq'
dikhla ke us ko aaina toDa kare koi
surat aur aap se be-aib bashar ki surat
ye pairahan jo miri ruuh ka utar na saka
nil-gun jhiil naaf tak pahne
gustakhi-e-visal hai mashshata-e-niyaz
tamam bazm hai kya mahv us ki baton men
taammul to tha un ko aane men qasid
tum ne sirf chaha hai ham ne chhu ke dekhe hain
kahne ko dil ki baat jinhen DhunDte the ham
vo jo kabutar us mukhe men rahte the kis des uDe
us dil-aram ko mutlaq kabhi aram na ho
vo shokh rang bhi dhime paDe havaon men
dastan vo thi jise dil bujhte bujhte kah gaya
ai jan-e-'faraz' itni bhi taufiq kise thi
yahi dariya hai jis men mauj ko sahil nahin milta
hangama ik pari ki savari ka dekhna
ham ne nahin kya vo kaam haan ba-khuda nahin kiya
ho sake tum se to kuchh haath baTao yaaro
paani ko ab to sar se guzar jaana chahiye
vahi gosha-e-qafas hai vahi fasl-e-gul ka matam
hue jo aankh se ojhal to baar baar dikhe
koi rasta sada dushvar ho aisa nahin hota
gai yak-ba-yak jo hava palaT nahin dil ko mere qarar hai
dilon men aag khushi se lagai jaati hai
ansuon se in ankhon men aate rahe
ek muddat se tiri yaad bhi aai na hamen
sab miTa den dil se hain jitni ki us men khvahishen
jo bahar thi so khizan hui jo khizan thi ab vo bahar hai
un ko kya kya ghussa aaya nazmon ke unvanon par
jo rah-e-ishq men qadam rakkhen
pasina tausan-e-himmat to sail-e-khana-e-zin hai
khalish-e-ghamza-e-khun-rez na puchh
tere qamat se bhi lipTi hai amar-bel koi
siipi men ban na paae guhar tum ko is se kya

pir-e-haram ne kaha sun ke meri ruedad
shumal-e-javedan-e-sabz-e-jan se
ik barg-e-nabat ho gai hai
mujhe na sun mire be-shakl ab dikhai to de
par javani ham ko yaad aai bahut

be-khudi be-sabab nahin 'ghalib'
the ye hi do hisab so yuun paak ho gae
apne ji men zara to kar insaf
siine men dil ki aahaT jaise koi jasus chale
daman daman dhuul

kahta tha nasehon se mire munh na aaiyo
naseho pand-garo rahguzar to dekho
zakhm ke bharte talak nakhun na baDh javenge kya

kar ke bimar di dava tu ne
intiha ishq ki khuda jaane
vahi naz-o-ada vahi ghamze
afaq ki manzil se gaya kaun salamat
us se zara sa rabt baDhana bahut hua
us ne pheri nigah-e-naz ye maalum hua
ye mujhe chain kyuun nahin paDta
pur huun main shikve se yuun raag se jaise baaja
ho ke ik baar samna un se

ji khush to ho gaya magar aansu nikal paDe
doston se to ashnai gai

auron ke bal pe bal na kar itna na chal nikal

charon janib sannaTa hai divane yaad aate hain
ham ne bhi ik shahr men rah kar thoDa naam kamaya hai
shikayat-ha-e-rangin ka gila kya
ashkon ke tabassum men aahon ke tarannum men

abhi phir ruuTh jaenge abhi to man ke baiThe hain

dard ko be-dava kiya tu ne

yaani ki dil ke jaane ka matam bahut hai yaan
tu vo nahin ki tujh ko tamasha kare koi

tire khayal ko kuchh chup si lag gai varna

ilaj-e-dard men bhi dard ki lazzat pe marta huun

hamen parva nahin ham se agar vo tan ke baiThe hain

vo masiha-nafas nahin jis ka

meri qismat se khelne vaale

ham to sirat-pasand ashiq hain

ek bachche ki tarah se use hansta dekhun
ki nukta-ha-e-khudi hain misal-e-tegh-e-asil
mujh sine ka ghubar khoti hai
ham se puchho 'jigar' ki sar-masti
Dhala hi jaata huun furqat men dopahar ki tarah
ham se khul jaao ba-vaqt-e-mai-parasti ek din
raste men vo mila tha main bach kar guzar gaya
jo kiya us ne raqam bhulne vaala hai kahin
kya gila sang-dil zamane ka
rahm kar zalim ki kya bud-e-charagh-e-kushta hai
main ik ik tiir pe khud Dhaal banti
tira dil to hai sanam-ashna tujhe kya milega namaz men

roz kahte hain aap aaj nahin

bakhshish ne mujh ko abr-e-karam ki kiya khajil
faza ko jaise koi raag chirta jaae
ya subh-dam jo dekhiye aa kar to bazm men

abhi zinda huun lekin sochta rahta huun khalvat men
is dasht-e-be-samar se juz khaak kuchh na paaya
kisi ke vada-e-farda ki guftugu hi sahi
phir kaun bhala dad-e-tabassum unhen dega
man ki duniya men na paaya main ne afrangi ka raaj
ye sadi dushman-e-arbab-e-hunar lagti hai
sahra ke us taraf se gae saare karvan
nashv-o-numa hai asl se 'ghalib' furua ko
koi roTi jo khaDa maang raha hai mujh se
khatm hai ulfat ki tujh par parda-dari haae haae
parinda kyuun kisi shakh-e-shajar ka ho jaae
kya raushan ho jaati thi gali jab yaar hamara guzre tha
jo tire dil ko lubhae vo ada mujh men nahin
dil ko dard-ashna kiya tu ne
sulagti ret men paani kahan tha
nahin is khuli faza men koi gosha-e-faraghat
jab tak ki na dekha tha qad-e-yar ka aalam
nafas ne maujon ka jaal bun kar lahad ne kam-e-nahang ho kar
ab kya bataen kaun tha kya tha vo ek shakhs
janab-e-hazrat-e-naseh ka vaah kya kahna
zar hi main dene laaya huun jaan aap dijiye
phir to ye uncha hi hota jaega
alfaz parakhta rahta hai
rahm kar apni tamanna par ki kis mushkil men hai
chhoD kar us ko tiri bazm se kyunkar jaun
kis ke ghar jaega sailab-e-bala mere baad
tu yahin haar gaya hai mire buzdil dushman
dam-ba-dam bharte hain ham teri hava-khvahi ka dam
daulat-e-duniya nahin jaane ki hargiz tere saath
ham ko dava-e-ishq-bazi hai
gosh minnat-kash-e-gulbang-e-tasalli na hua
na jo kaaTe se kaTti hai na jo Taale se Talti hai
ye ghurbaten miri ankhon men kaisi utri hain
vahshaten bhi nazar aati hain sar-e-parda-e-naz
hue tum dost jis ke dushman us ka asman kyuun ho
ab shauq se bigaD ke hi baten kiya karo
khuun se sinchi hai main ne jo zamin mar mar ke
zer-e-shamshir-e-sitam 'mir' taDapna kaisa
avaz de raha huun vafa ke maqam se
taghaful-ha-e-tamkin-azma kya
bala se hain jo ye pesh-e-nazar dar-o-divar
vabal-e-bal-o-par hai aur main huun
ki angare pe hoga charagar ruui se kya hasil
dekh gharat-gar-e-dil ye bhi khazane mere
gada samajh ke vo chup tha miri jo shamat aai
aankh jhapki hi thi ki raat gai
ab-e-haivan pila diya tu ne
tu fath-mand mera tarasha hua sanam
koi ye puchhe ki vaaiz ka kya bigaDta hai
us ko taqaza rava mujh pe taqaza haram
aur kya is se ziyada koi narmi bartun
sabad-e-gul ke tale band kare hai gulchin
dil o jaan fida-e-rahe kabhi aa ke dekh hamdam
hai mira virana-e-gham 'nazm' aisa haul-nak
kahan vo shokh mulaqat khud se bhi na hui
khayal-e-marg kab taskin dil-e-azurda ko bakhshe
ze-khud-raftagi-ha-e-hairat salamat
khud apne se milne ka to yaara na tha mujh men
ham to vo phuul jo shakhon pe ye sochen pahron
us ke giton se zamane ko sanvaren yaaro
un se pyaar kiya hai ham ne un ki raah men ham baiThe hain
rone ko nahin koi hansne ko zamana hai
phir main sunne laga huun is dil ki
jaana paDa raqib ke dar par hazar baar
us ke kuche men bhi ho raah se be-rah 'nasir'
ye dil ka daagh jo khud bhi bhala lage hai mujhe
nahin ishq men is ka to ranj hamen ki qarar o shakeb zara na raha
sehn-e-gulshan men kabhi ai shah-e-shamshad-qadan
jaate to ho par ham se Tuk aankh mila jaana
